On Transcription Faithfulness in Vision-Language Models","abstract":"Vision Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly used in place of traditional OCR pipelines for document understanding. In this paper, we show they do not always act as faithful transcribers: when text is imperfect, they often tend to rewrite it into a more plausible form - a behavior that clean-text OCR benchmarks cannot detect. We introduce FaithC4, a multilingual perturbation benchmark of 1,455 single-page documents (English, Chinese, Korean) with three perturbation families: scramble, random s","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-29T20:33:35Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2607_21617.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究的是一个很实际但常被忽略的问题：VLM 在文档转写时，究竟是在“读”图像里的字，还是在用语言先验“改写”成更像正常文本的内容。标准 OCR benchmark 多是干净文本，测不出这种偏差；而一旦输入里有拼写错误、字符扰动或视觉歧义，通用 VLM 可能输出更合理但不忠实的文本。作者要解决的核心问题不是 OCR 准确率本身，而是 transcription faithfulness，也就是模型是否逐字忠实于视觉证据。","importance":"对纯 LLM 预训练的直接相关性有限，但对多模态预训练和统一建模很有启发。它揭示了一个典型的 pretrain-posttrain 张力：强语言建模先验会提升语义恢复能力，却可能损害字面保真。对于把文档理解、图像文字读取、视觉 token 到文本解码统一进一个大模型的人，这个问题很关键，因为它不是下游 prompt 小问题，而是训练目标与模态融合方式带来的系统性偏置。更具体地说，这篇工作提醒两点。第一，VLM 里的 LM prior 不是免费午餐，尤其在需要 literal transcription 的场景。第二，评测如果只看 clean OCR，会高估通用 VLM 作为“读字器”的可靠性。贡献主要在 benchmark 与现象刻画，不是新训练方法。","technical_approach":"技术路线分两部分：构造可控扰动 benchmark，以及用它分离“视觉读取”与“语言改写”两种行为。作者从 mC4 采样英文、中文、韩文单页文档文本，先在文本层做受控字符扰动，再渲染成 PDF 图像。这样 ground truth 就是扰动后的文本，而不是原始干净文本，因此可以明确判断模型是在忠实转写，还是偷偷纠正。这个设计比直接在现成扫描文档上评测更干净，因为扰动位置、比例、类型都可控。\n\n扰动分三类。Scramble 保留首尾字符、打乱中间字符，用来测试模型是否依赖词形整体模式和语言先验；Random 用同脚本随机字符替换，测试强破坏下的鲁棒性；Visual 用视觉相似字符替换，测试视觉歧义与语言先验的竞争。英文和韩文按词处理，中文按 4 字窗口处理。作者在 8% 的 eligible words 或字符窗口上施加扰动，并保留原始版本作为对照。\n\n评测对象分三类：通用 VLM、OCR-specialized VLM、传统 OCR。这个分组很重要，因为它对应不同强度的语言先验。通用 VLM 经过大规模多模态与文本预训练，最容易“脑补”；OCR 专项模型经过文档抽取微调，语言先验被任务约束部分压制；传统 OCR 基本是字符级识别，没有强生成式 prior。作者用统一 prompt 让通用 VLM 自然输出，不额外强调“不要纠错”，避免把现象用指令压掉。\n\n除了整体误差统计，论文还对 Qwen3-VL-4B 做层间 probing，分析何时发生 rewriting。作者发现，当扰动词的内部表示仍靠近原始词编码时，模型更容易走向“改写”而非“照抄”。另一个细节是词长效应：短词更容易被语言模型先验覆盖，长词一旦偏离正常拼写，模型反而不容易自信地纠正。","experiments":"实验规模是 1,455 份单页文档，覆盖 EN/ZH/KO 三种脚本，评测 15 个系统。主结果很清楚：通用 VLM 在扰动下的 WER 退化最大，英文上可到 4.5 个百分点；OCR-specialized VLM 的退化约 0.2–2 个百分点；传统 OCR 小于 0.6 个百分点。这个差异基本支持作者的核心论点：语言先验越强，越容易在 imperfect text 上牺牲忠实性。\n\n另一个重要发现是非局部效应。即便只扰动约 5% 的词，通用 VLM 在未扰动部分上的错误也会比 clean baseline 高 5–10 倍。这说明 rewriting 不是局部字符识别失误，而是整页上下文建模被带偏，模型会在更大范围内重写文本。对统一文档模型来说，这个现象比单点 WER 更值得警惕。\n\n跨语言结果也有信息量。结论在英中韩都成立，但最伤的扰动类型因脚本不同而异，说明 rewriting 既受语言模型先验影响，也受书写系统和字符空间结构影响。中文没有 scramble，更多体现 visual/random 替换的影响；韩文由于可施加扰动的长词比例较低，整体扰动率也更低。\n\n机制分析部分，Qwen3-VL-4B 的 layerwise probing 显示：当 perturbed word 的表示与原词仍接近时，模型更可能输出原词或“合理词”，而不是视觉上真实出现的扰动词。词长实验显示 4–6 字符短词的 rewriting 率可达约 10%，超过 7 字符后降到 0%。这说明 LM prior 对短词的覆盖更强，而长词的组合空间更大，模型不容易做高置信纠错。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读到中等深度。先看 Introduction，抓住作者要测的是 faithfulness，不是一般 OCR accuracy。然后看数据构造部分，确认 ground truth 为什么定义成“扰动后文本”，以及三类 perturbation 分别在测什么。主表格里重点比较三类系统在不同语言、不同扰动下的 WER 退化，而不是只看 clean performance。接着看 non-local effect 的分析，这部分最能说明通用 VLM 的问题不是局部识别，而是整页生成偏置。最后看 Qwen3-VL-4B 的 probing 和词长分析，理解 rewriting 何时发生。\n\n如果只花 25–35 actually read the text, or do they rewrite it into something more plausible? On clean benchmarks, this distinction is mostly invisible. But once the input contains typos, character corruption, or visually ambiguous symbols, a model with a strong language prior may output a corrected or normalized string rather than the literal content in the image. The core problem is therefore transcription faithfulness, not generic OCR accuracy.","importance":"Its direct relevance to text-only pretraining is limited, but it is quite relevant to multimodal pretraining and unified modeling. The paper exposes a clear trade-off: stronger language-model priors can improve semantic recovery while hurting literal fidelity. That matters for document VLMs, OCR-capable multimodal LMs, and any setup that decodes text from visual tokens using a powerful generative backbone. The main lesson is not about prompting; it is about objective mismatch and modality fusion. If evaluation only measures clean-text OCR, it can overestimate how reliable a general-purpose VLM is as a faithful reader. consist","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-03T19:21:06Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2607_21600.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是多模态大模型里一个很具体、也很难靠传统输入过滤解决的问题：攻击者可以把恶意意图拆散到图像和文本两侧，使得单看任一模态都显得正常，但融合后模型会走向有害响应。论文要解决的不是一般意义上的“检测不安全输出”，而是识别多模态融合过程本身是否出现了异常的信息流。作者的判断是，很多多模态 objective 的辅助约束。贡献量级偏方法性和分析性，不是训练范式的改写，但对理解 MLLM 的跨模态耦合失效有一定信息增益。","technical_approach":"方法核心很简单：对同一个图文输入，分别跑 text-only、vision-only、multimodal 三种推理配置，取它们的预测分布，再从这些分布之间构造一组低维特征，作者称为 FlowVector。FlowVector 试图近似刻画三类关系。第一类是 fused prediction 相对各单模态预测的偏移，反映某个模态是否异常地主导了最终决策；第二类是两个单模态预测之间的一致性，反映共享语义是否对齐；第三类是融合后不确定性是下降还是上升，反映 fusion 是在消歧还是在放大冲突。论文把这些量解释为对 unique / redundant / synergistic information 的信息论代理量，但实现上并没有真的做严格 PID，而是用 KL、JS、熵变化之类可计算统计量来近似。检测器部分采用 one-class classification，只用 benign 数据学习“正常融合流形”，推理时把偏离该流形的样本判为可疑。这个设计有两个明确取舍：一是不改底座模型，不需要对抗训练；二是把泛化寄托在 benign manifold 建模上，而不是覆盖攻击分布。与已有方法相比，区别主要在于它不看 raw input pattern，也不依赖生成后 verifier 对输出做重审，而是把检测点放在模型内部可观测的分布行为上。代价则是每个样本要额外做多次前向，且依赖模型能稳定提供三种配置下可比的 token-level 或 sequence-level 分布。","experiments":"实验结论比较集中：在多个 MLLM 和多种未见过的 multimodal jailbreak 设定下，FlowGuard 能把攻击成功率从 90% 以上压到 15% 以下，同时 benign utility 相对基线损失控制在 3% 以内，推理开销上比 diffusion-based verification 快约 6 倍。论文强调的是 zero-shot / unseen attack detection，也就是训练时不需要攻击样本监督。更值得看的不是 headline 数字，而是几组消融。其一，单独使用输入或输出层面的置信度信号效果明显不如 FlowVector，说明异常主要出在融合关系而不是单点不确定性。其二，若去掉 text-only 或 vision-only 分支，只看 multimodal 自身统计，检测性能会明显下降，说明三路对照是必要的。其三，one-class benign-only 训练在跨攻击泛化上优于基于已知攻击训练的二分类器，支持作者关于“攻击分布难穷尽，正常分布更可建模”的论点。摘要里给出的数字已经覆盖主结果；全文如果有附表，建议重点核对不同模型、不同攻击类型下 ASR 和 benign pass rate 的波动，判断方法是否依赖某类架构或某类视觉编码器。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction mi","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-24T17:59:22Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2607_22529.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决的是 LLM 自博弈训练中的任务生成质量问题：如何在没有人工标注、又不被固定环境限制的情况下，持续生成多样、可验证、难度合适的训练任务。已有 self-play 依赖外部环境时验证可靠，但任务空间窄；完全开放的任务生成加后验过滤时任务多样，但容易生成不成立的题、模板化题，并在多轮迭代中出现合成数据退化。","importance":"这篇论文更接近 post-train / self-improvement，但对基座预训练研究也有启发。核心不是某个工具调用 benchmark 的提升，而是提出一种训练信号治理方式：把自生成任务中的结构知识压缩成可复用、可路由、可演化的 skill package。它对应的是数据生成器的状态管理问题。对 RL pretrain、持续学习和 synthetic data scaling 都有参考意义：如果只把历史轨迹塞进 prompt，上下文会膨胀，控制力会下降；如果只靠过滤器，错误只会被拒绝，不会反向塑造生成分布。Skill-SP 把任务模式、验证机制和课程难度放进同一个可演化接口，试图让模型沿着自身能力边界持续产出训练样本。","technical_approach":"Skill-SP 由三个角色和一个技能库组成。Proposer 根据路由到的 skill package 合成挑战任务；Solver 负责解题并通过强化学习更新；Controller 读取执行轨迹，维护技能库。skill package 是模块化的程序性知识单元，包含任务说明、支持资源、结构约束、可执行验证机制和历史难度信息。它不是简单的 prompt 模板，而是 self-play 循环中的控制接口。Controller 会根据反馈改写已有技能、归档过时技能，并从开放探索中归纳新技能。这样的设计解决两个问题。第一，结构先验在任务生成前注入，可以减少无效任务，而不是生成后再被动筛掉。第二，验证机制随 skill 一起携带，使任务生成和结果判定共享同一个模式定义，降低题目不可判定或答案不唯一的风险。与 unguided self-play 相比，它多了显式的技能路由、技能演化和难度跟踪；与固定环境 self-play 相比，它不把任务分布锁死在单一模拟器或单一 verifier 内。","experiments":"实验覆盖两类可验证 agent 任务：工具调用和逻辑推理。工具调用使用 API-Bank 和 BFCL，逻辑推理使用 ZebraLogic。论文在多个开源 LLM backbone 上验证，并在图中展示了 Qwen3-4B-Ins 的能力 footprint。结果显示，Skill-SP 相比基线有明显绝对增益：工具使用任务最高提升 +42.9 分，逻辑推理最高提升 +12.0 分，并持续优于 unguided self-play。文中还指出 unguided self-play 在逻辑推理设置下无法稳定合成有效 puzzle，因此没有进入对应比较。诊断实验强调两个现象：Skill-SP 能把新出现的任务模式沉淀成技能包；技能路由和难度历史能让任务更贴近模型当前学习边界，而不是长期停留在过易模板或漂移到不可解噪声。","reading_guide":"建议精读方法部分的 skill package 定义、Proposer-Solver-Controller 循环、Controller 的技能更新规则，以及任务验证接口。实验部分重点看 API-Bank、BFCL、ZebraLogic 三组结果和与 unguided self-play 的对照。诊断分析比单个 benchmark 分数更重要，尤其是技能库如何增长、技能如何被归档或重写、任务难度如何随训练轮次变化。还需要检查 RL 更新细节：Solver 的训练目标、奖励来源、失败样本处理、是否存在 verifier 偏置。预计精读需要 1.5 到 2 小时；如果只关心机制，先看框架图、算法框和诊断实验即可。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a central failure mode in LLM self-play: how to generate diverse, valid, verifiable, and appropriately difficult tasks without human supervision. Self-play grounded in external environments has reliable verification but a narrow task distribution. Open-ended task generation with post-hoc filtering has broader coverage, but it often produces ill-posed tasks, repetitive templates, and accumulated synthetic-data errors over training iterations.","importance":"The work is a post-training and self-improvement paper, but its main idea is relevant to pre-training research through the lens of training-signal governance. The key contribution is not merely better tool-use accuracy. It proposes a way to maintain state in a synthetic task generator by distilling reusable task patterns into modular skill packages. This is useful for thinking about RL pre-training, continual learning, and synthetic data scaling. Historical trajectories alone are too verbose to serve as a stable generator memory, and passive filters only reject bad samples after generation. Skill-SP instead uses skills as an active interface for generation, verification, and curriculum control.","technical_approach":"Skill-SP consists of a Proposer, a Solver, a Controller, and an evolving skill library. The Proposer generates challenges conditioned on routed skill packages. The Solver attempts the tasks and is trained through reinforcement learning. The Controller analyzes execution traces and updates the skill library. A skill package is a modular unit of procedural knowledge. It may contain task instructions, supporting resources, structural constraints, executable verification logic, and historical difficulty statistics. This makes it more than a prompt template: it is a control surface for the self-play loop. The Controller can refine existing skills, archive obsolete ones, and induce new skills from open-ended exploration. Compared with unguided self-play, Skill-SP adds explicit routing, skill evolution, and difficulty tracking. Compared with environment-specific self-play, it avoids tying the task distribution to a single simulator or verifier.","experiments":"The paper evaluates on two families of verifiable agent tasks. Tool calling is tested on API-Bank and BFCL, while logical reasoning is tested on ZebraLogic. Experiments are run across multiple open-source LLM backbones, and the introduction highlights a Qwen3-4B-Instruct footprint. Skill-SP reports absolute gains of up to +42.9 points on tool use and +12.0 points on logical reasoning, consistently outperforming unguided self-play. The paper also notes that unguided self-play fails to synthesize valid puzzles in the logical-reasoning setting, so it is absent from that comparison. Diagnostic analyses indicate that the method can capture newly discovered task patterns as reusable skills and keep generated tasks closer to the model’s moving learning frontier.","reading_guide":"Read the method sections defining skill packages, the Proposer-Solver-Controller loop, the Controller update rules, and the verification interface. The diagnostic section is more informative than the headline scores: it should show whether the skill library actually grows, when skills are rewritten or archived, and how task difficulty changes across iterations. Also check the RL details, including the Solver objective, reward construction, treatment of failed samples, and possible verifier bias. Existing methods typically retain or discard tokens based solely on the magnitude of their importance ratios, applying the same threshold uniformly across token positions. In this work, we reveal that the natural scale of the importance r","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-24T10:55:42Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2607_22186.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是异步 RL 在大模型后训练里的稳定性问题。异步训练通过并行 rollout 生成和策略更新显著提高吞吐，但也带来 stale/off-policy 数据：用于更新的轨迹并不是由当前策略完整生成的，长轨迹里甚至可能跨越多个权重版本。现有方法通常按 importance ratio 的绝对大小统一裁剪或屏蔽 token，默认“大 ratio 就不可信”。作者指出这个前提在异步 agentic RL 里并不成立，因为 ratio 的自然尺度随 token entropy 系统变化。低熵位置的大 ratio 往往只是近零概率放大的采样噪声，高熵位置的大 ratio 反而可能是异步更新带来的真实探索信号。核心问题因此变成：如何在不重建精确行为策略的情况下，区分应当抑制的 off-policy 噪声和应当保留的有效探索。","importance":"这篇论文的价值不只在一个更稳的异步 RL 技巧，而在于它把 off-policy ratio 的解释从“统一阈值的几何裁剪”推进到“与熵耦合的状态依赖信任域”。这对大模型 RL 很关键，因为长 CoT、多轮 agent rollout、异步 pipeline 都在把训练推向更强的 off-policy 区域。若这个问题处理不好，吞吐提升会被训练崩溃抵消。对 pretrain 研究者而言，启发在两层。第一，它揭示了 token-level 训练信号的尺度不是常数，而与分布熵相关，这和预训练里 loss spike、难度分布、confidence calibration 的分析是相通的。第二，它说明异步系统设计不能只看系统吞吐，必须把优化目标里的统计失真一并建模。贡献量级上，这属于“机制解释 + 方法落地”都比较完整的一类工作：既解释了为什么现有 ratio clipping/masking 会错杀或误放，又给出一个不依赖行为策略重建的替代方案，并在强基线上拿到接近同步训练的效果。","technical_approach":"技术主线有三步。第一步，作者建立 entropy-ratio relation。通过实证和附录推导，他们说明 token 的 log-importance-ratio 或 ratio 偏移量的自然尺度会随 token entropy 增长。直观上，若一个 token 处于低熵、模型极其自信的区域，行为策略和目标策略之间哪怕只有很小的 train-inference gap，也会因为某些近零概率项而被放大成很大的 ratio；这种大值并不一定对应真实策略漂移，而更像数值上被放大的采样噪声。相反，在高熵区域，策略本来就不确定，较大的 ratio 更可能落在合理波动范围内。第二步，作者分析异步 RL 的 staleness 结构，不再把一条轨迹视为来自单一行为策略，而是拆成 inter-trajectory staleness 和 intra-trajectory version switching。后者是文章里更关键的观察：长轨迹生成过程中，worker 可能在中途同步到新权重，于是同一条轨迹前半段和后半段来自不同版本。此时在切换点附近，当前目标策略去评估旧前缀下的新续写，会同时看到 entropy 和 ratio 的跃升。作者认为这不是应被统一裁掉的“坏 off-policy”，而是异步训练天然引入的探索。第三步，在上述分析上提出 ESTR，Entropy-Scaled Trust Region。它不是用固定阈值判断 |δ_t| 是否过大，而是让每个 token 的允许边界随其熵自适应伸缩：低熵 token 采用更紧的 trust region，避免噪声放大进入梯度；高熵 token 采用更宽的 trust region，保留真实探索。论文强调 ESTR 不需要显式重建行为策略，也不依赖人为 magnitude bias。实现上，它是 token-level 的筛选/约束机制，可嵌入异步 RL 流程中，替代传统的统一 ratio clipping 或 hard masking。","experiments":"实验重点放在长时程 agentic 任务和复杂推理 benchmark。文中给出的代表性结果是：在 BrowseComp-Plus 上，ESTR 达到 37.3 avg@1，优于最强异步基线的 34.9；同时训练速度达到同步 GRPO 的 2.6 倍，并能在效果上匹配同步 GRPO。这个结果很关键，因为异步方法常见的两难是“更快但更差”或“更稳但吞吐损失大”，而 ESTR 试图把两者拉回平衡。论文的说服力主要来自几类证据。其一，真实 rollout 上的联合分布分析显示 |δ_t| 与 H_t 明显正相关，说明统一阈值缺乏统计依据。其二，版本切换点附近 entropy 和 ratio 同时上升，而标准化偏差项保持在正常尺度，支持“高熵大 ratio 未必是坏样本”的解释。其三，和 interval clipping、hard masking 以及其他异步稳定化方法相比，ESTR 在长轨迹场景下更稳，能减少 policy collapse。若精读实验，建议特别留意不同熵区间下 token 保留/抑制行为的可视化，以及同步、异步、不同 masking 策略之间的训练曲线差异。这些图比单个最终分数更能说明方法为何有效。","reading_guide":"这篇适合精读。先看 Introduction 和 Motivation，抓住作者要推翻的两个默认前提：ratio 大小可直接代表可信度，以及一条轨迹可视作来自单一行为策略。随后重点读 Figure 1 到 Figure 3 对应的分析：一是 entropy 与 |δ_t| 的耦合关系，二是 intra-trajectory version switching 如何造成切换点处的联合尖峰。方法部分要看清 ESTR 的边界如何随熵变化，以及它在训练中具体替代了哪种 clipping/masking 逻辑。实验部分优先看 BrowseComp-Plus 和其他长轨迹任务上的主结果表，再看训练稳定性曲线和按熵分桶的分析图。附录若有时间值得读，因为理论推导决定了这篇工作的可信度，不只是经验技巧。精读时可以带着三个问题：第一，熵是否是最合适的尺度变量，还是还应结合 advantage/reward uncertainty；第二，ESTR 与 PPO/GRPO 的 clipping 是否存在相互作用；第三，这种 token-level trust region 能否迁移到异步 pretraining、speculative decoding 产生的分布偏移，或多模态 token 的统一 RL 场景。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"$f","importance":"The contribution matters beyond one more stabilization trick. It changes how off-policy ratios should be interpreted: not as quantities with a universal threshold, but as state-dependent signals whose scale is entropy-conditioned. That is important for long-CoT RL, multi-turn agents, and any high-throughput asynchronous pipeline, where stale data are unavoidable. For pretraining researchers, the broader lesson is that token-level training signals do not live on a fixed scale; their meaning depends on local uncertainty. This resonates with issues such as confidence calibration, loss spikes, and optimization under heterogeneous token difficulty. The paper is also useful because it connects systems design and optimization statistics directly: throughput gains from asynchrony are only real if the induced distribution shift is modeled correctly. In terms of contribution type, this is a strong combination of mechanism analysis and practical method design.","technical_approach":"$10","experiments":"$11","reading_guide":"$12","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"应读；它改变 importance-ratio clipping 的默认先验，异步 RL 系统要复查低熵 token 的噪声放大。","en":"Read it; it changes the default prior on importance-ratio clipping, and async RL systems should recheck low-entropy token noise amplification."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Guanqun Zhao","Zijun Xie","Binbin Zheng","Enlei Gong","Jiafeng Lu","Yehan Yang","Aoqi Hu","Zeyu Chen"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["asynchronous-RL","importance-ratio","trust-region","RLHF","training-stability"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22186","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.22186","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.21856","title":"LeAct: Learning to Reason from Expert Actions","abstract":"Modern reasoning models depend on reasoning data, today sourced from human annotations or distilled from stronger LLMs. However, a rich and largely untapped source of supervision lies in expert systems (e.g., game engines, classical planners, theorem provers), which routinely produce near-optimal actions across diverse domains. But these experts are silent: they commit to an action without writing down the chain of thought (CoT) behind it. Recovering that CoT as natural-language reasoning would ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-23T22:50:23Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2607_21856.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在只有“专家动作/策略”监督、没有自然语言推理过程（CoT）的情况下，如何从 action-only expert 中恢复出对学生模型“因果有用”的推理文本，并用它训练出更会泛化的 reason-then-act 模型，同时避免常见的 rationalization（看似合理但与决策无关的解释）。","importance":"对预训练不是直接方法学贡献，但对 post-train/推理数据构造有明确启发：把非语言专家（CFR、规划器、控制器、RL policy）的动作当作强监督信号，通过闭环筛选生成“能提高行动概率”的 CoT，从而得到更高信噪比的推理数据。对做基础模型的人，价值在于两点：1）提供了一个不依赖更强 LLM 的 CoT 来源，潜在可扩展到大量可验证/可求解环境；2）把‘CoT 是否真的帮助决策’变成可优化的训练信号，而不是只用答案对错或表面可读性做过滤。","technical_approach":"LeAct 把 CoT 视为连接状态 x 与专家动作 y* 的潜变量 z，并显式建模学生的 reason-then-act 分解：pθ(z,y|x)=pθ(z|x)pθ(y|x,z)。训练数据只有 (x, π*(·|x))。\n\n关键做法是用“反向提案 + 重要性加权”的闭环来选 CoT：\n- 反向提案 q_bwd(z|x,y)：给定 (x,y) 让模型生成多条候选 CoT（相当于从动作反推理由）。\n- 用 IWAE bound（N-sample importance-weighted bound）推导训练梯度：对每个候选 z_i 计算权重 w_i = pθ(z_i,y|x) / q_bwd(z_i|x,y)，归一化后得到软权重 ~w_i，对 (z_i,y) 做加权 SFT 更新。直观上，这一步在做“E-step 式的解释选择”：保留那些能显著提高学生在给定 CoT 时预测专家动作概率 pθ(y|x,z) 的解释。\n- M-step 目标是拟合专家策略 π*：当专家给出分布时可闭式期望；只给单动作时退化为 Dirac。\n\n与直接让 LLM 写 CoT 的差别在于：LeAct 的过滤标准不是‘解释像不像’，而是‘解释是否让模型更能复现专家动作’，从而抑制 rationalization。与一些 latent-EM 自训练（如 proposal-and-score）相似，但这里 latent 被外部专家动作锚定，而不是被模型自生成文本锚定。","experiments":"论文覆盖三类专家来源：\n- CFR solver：在可枚举的小型不完全信息扑克（如 Leduc Hold’em）上，LeAct 训练的学生可以匹配专家 solver。\n- DeepCFR policy：在更大规模的 Flop Hold’em（文中提到约 10^9 infosets）上，LeAct 优于 behavior cloning 与 expert-iteration 基线，并在未见状态上保持优势。\n- 机器人模拟：在 cube-stacking 基准上，LeAct 是唯一能超过 direct imitation 的配方。\n\n作者强调消融：把“解释选择/排序”替换为随机排序会抹掉收益，说明闭环筛选是主要增益来源。摘要未给出具体数值表格；若要抓数字，需要直接看实验章节的胜率/回报/成功率与样本量设置。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 30–45 分钟）：\n- 先看 Introduction 里对 rationalization 的动机与闭环过滤的直觉解释，确认它解决的是‘解释是否因果有用’而不是‘解释是否好看’。\n- 重点读 Method：IWAE bound 到“加权 SFT 更新”的推导是核心；理解 w_i 里 pθ(z_i,y|x) 与 q_bwd 的角色，能把算法和 EM/重要性采样联系起来。\n- 再看实验的消融表：尤其是随机排序/去掉筛选后的退化幅度，用来判断方法是否稳健。\n\n如果时间有限：只读方法图（pipeline）+ 关键消融 + 一个大规模任务（DeepCFR Flop Hold’em）的主结果即可。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"How can we recover natural-language reasoning traces (CoT) from action-only experts (solvers/planners/policies) that never expose their internal reasoning, and train a reason-then-act model with CoTs that are causally useful for decision making rather than post-hoc rationalizations?","importance":"Not a pretraining-method paper, but a strong post-training/data-construction idea for reasoning: it turns non-linguistic experts into a scalable supervision source without relying on a stronger LLM to distill CoT. The key contribution is making “does this CoT actually help the model choose the expert action?” the filtering signal, instead of answer correctness or surface plausibility. This can raise the signal-to-noise ratio of synthetic reasoning data and is potentially extensible to many verifiable/simulatable domains.","technical_approach":"$13","experiments":"They evaluate on three expert families:\n- CFR solvers on small enumerable imperfect-information poker (e.g., Leduc Hold’em): the LeAct-trained student can match the expert.\n- DeepCFR policy on large Flop Hold’em (~10^9 infosets mentioned): LeAct outperforms behavior cloning and expert-iteration baselines and generalizes better to unseen states.\n- Simulated robotics cube-stacking: LeAct is the only recipe that improves over direct imitation.\n\nAblations highlight that replacing the explanation-selection step with random ranking removes the gains, indicating the selection/filtering is the primary driver. (For exact win-rate/success-rate numbers, the main experimental tables in §5–6 are the place to look.)","reading_guide":"Skim plan (~30–45 minutes):\n- Introduction: focus on the rationalization failure mode and why action-likelihood is a better filter than plausibility.\n- Method section: the IWAE-to-weighted-SFT derivation is the technical core; understand the role of q_bwd and the importance weights.\n- Experiments: prioritize the ablation tables (random ranking / no filtering) and one large-scale setting (DeepCFR Flop Hold’em) to gauge robustness.\n\nIf time is tight, read the pipeline figure + the key ablations + the main results on the largest game.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读方法部分即可；把 action likelihood 当作 CoT 过滤器的 setup 干净，适合迁移到 verifier/RL 数据合成管线。","en":"Skim the method section; using action likelihood as a CoT filter is a clean setup and maps naturally to verifier- or RL-based reasoning data pipelines."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Princeton University"],"authors":["Ziran Yang","Chengshuai Shi","Raj Ghugare","Benjamin Eysenbach","Karthik Narasimhan","Chi Jin"],"notable_authors":["Karthik Narasimhan","Chi Jin","Benjamin Eysenbach"],"tags":["reasoning-data","latent-cot","expert-actions","post-training","reinforcement-learning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21856","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.21856","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.21971","title":"Teaching LLMs to Self-Evolve: Cultivating Core Meta-Skills with Reinforcement Learning","abstract":"Test-time scaling through iterative self-evolution with environment feedback, as demonstrated by AlphaEvolve, shows remarkable performance gains. We hypothesize that the success of such evolution frameworks hinges on meta-skills, such as self-reflection with environment feedback, that enable effective multi-round refinement, yet are largely neglected by traditional post-training. To bridge this gap, we present MetaEvolve, a framework designed to develop these meta-skills via a data synthesis However, GRPO relies on repeated generation of long chain-of-thought rollouts. Training time scales with the number of rollouts, a large fraction of which are uninformative. Thus, GRPO is computationally expensive and unstable. To mitigate this, existing approaches either generate a larger pool of rollou","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-24T06:04:04Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2607_22002.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 RLVR/GRPO 里一个很具体但代价很高的问题：每个 prompt 固定采样很多条 rollout，其中相当一部分样本组几乎没有奖励方差，因而对 GRPO 的相对优势估计和梯度更新贡献很小，却占掉了大部分生成算力。作者关心的不是再造一个新的 RL 目标，而是在不改 GRPO 基本范式的前提下，怎样把 rollout 预算更有选择地投到“当前最有学习信号”的样本上，尤其是在训练早期。","importance":"对预训练/后训练接口的启发主要有两点。第一，它把“数据选择”从 SFT 里的 loss/gradient 视角，转成了 RLVR 里更贴近目标函数的 reward-variance 视角，这对 reasoning RL 的数据效率问题是有参考价值的。第二，它强调训练早期的 rollout 分配比后期更关键，这和 pretrain 中 curriculum、cooldown、compute allocation 的思路是相通的。贡献量级上，这不是改变能力上限的范式工作，而是一个相对干净、工程上可落地的效率改进：不引入额外大池采样，不依赖历史统计做滞后筛选，也不改损失函数，重点是把同样的 rollout 预算用得更有效。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 VIGOR：Variance Guided Online Rollout allocation。作者先从二值奖励情形分析 GRPO 梯度，指出组内奖励方差直接决定了梯度信号强弱。直观上，如果一个 prompt 的多条 rollout 要么全对要么全错，那么组内相对优势几乎没有区分度；只有同一 prompt 下出现对错混合，GRPO 才能从组内比较中得到有效更新。基于这个观察，VIGOR 不再给所有 prompt 一次性分配固定 G 条 rollout，而是采用逐轮细化的在线分配。流程大致是：先给每个 prompt 一个较小的初始 rollout 数；根据当前已采样结果估计组级 reward variance；挑出方差最高、最可能继续产生信息增益的 prompt；再只对这些 prompt 追加 rollout；重复若干轮直到达到总预算。这样做和 DAPO 一类方法的区别在于，VIGOR 不是先为所有样本生成更大的候选池再过滤，而是在生成过程中边看信号边追加预算，因此避免了“为了筛选而额外生成”的开销。和利用 stale rollout 或辅助目标的方法相比，它也不改训练目标，仍然是标准 GRPO，只改 rollout allocation。文中还给出一个理论分析：在 Pareto 分布假设下，随着 refinement rounds 增加，相对标准 GRPO 的速度收益可以闭式刻画，并呈指数式增长趋势。这个理论更多是说明自适应分配在重尾信息分布下为什么有效，而不是严格预测真实训练曲线。","experiments":"实验覆盖数学推理和代码两类 RLVR 任务。数学部分使用 Qwen2.5-1.5B、3B、7B 和 Phi-4-Mini-Instruct，在 6 个 benchmark 上比较 rollout efficiency 和最终精度。结论比较稳定：在达到相同准确率时，VIGOR 需要更少 rollout；其中在 Qwen2.5-3B 上，rollout efficiency 最多提升 2.3 倍。若按公平的 rollout-matched 设置比较，也就是总 rollout 数相同，VIGOR 在四个模型设定上都拿到更高的平均分。代码部分在 LiveCodeBench v6 上用 Qwen3-8B，VIGOR 用 1.49 倍更少的 rollout 达到 GRPO 的最终 full pass rate，同时平均 test pass rate 还提升 3.4 个点。论文的关键说服力不只是最终分数，而是训练曲线：早期阶段同样预算下爬升更快，说明它确实把算力集中到了更有学习价值的样本上。值得注意的是，这类方法的收益通常依赖 reward variance 的长尾程度；如果任务本身大多数 prompt 都同样容易或同样困难，增益会收缩。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和方法图，抓住两个前提：rollout generation 是主要成本，且训练早期收益最大。然后重点看作者对二值奖励下 GRPO 梯度与组内 reward variance 关系的推导，这决定了方法是否站得住。实验部分建议优先看两类图：一类是 accuracy 对 rollout budget 的曲线，另一类是 rollout-matched 的最终结果表。前者回答“是否更省”，后者回答“同预算是否更强”。如果时间有限，30 到 40 分钟足够把核心思想和实验结论读清。若想深挖，可重点想两个问题：一是 reward variance 作为信息量代理在非二值、带格式奖励或代码单元测试奖励下是否仍然稳健；二是这种在线 allocation 能否和异步 RL、prompt-level curriculum 或 verifier uncertainty 结合。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific inefficiency in RLVR with GRPO: a fixed number of rollouts is generated for every prompt, but many prompt groups exhibit little or no reward variance, so they contribute almost no useful relative-advantage signal while consuming most of the generation budget. The goal is not to redesign the RL objective, but to allocate rollout compute to prompts that are actually informative at the current stage of training, especially early on.","importance":"For pretraining-oriented readers, the main value is conceptual rather than paradigm-shifting. It reframes data selection for reasoning RL around reward variance, instead of supervised proxies such as loss or gradient norm. That is a useful lens for data efficiency in RL-based post-training. It also reinforces a broader training principle familiar from pretraining: compute spent early, on the right examples, matters disproportionately. The contribution is best understood as a clean efficiency recipe: no extra candidate pool for filtering, no stale-rollout reuse trick, no auxiliary objective, and no change to GRPO itself.","technical_approach":"$16","experiments":"$17","reading_guide":"For a skim, start with the introduction and the main method figure. The two premises to keep in mind are: rollout generation dominates cost, and early training contributes disproportionately to final gains. Then read the derivation connecting GRPO gradients and within-group reward variance under binary rewards; that is the conceptual anchor of the method. In the experiments, prioritize the plots of accuracy versus rollout budget and the rollout-matched result tables. The former answers whether the method is more efficient, and the latter answers whether it is better under equal compute. Around 30–40 minutes should be enough for the main idea and evidence. imp","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-23T20:20:56Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2607_21793.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文试图缓解 reasoning model 在 RL 中出现的长 CoT 问题：模型为了提高最终正确率生成过长推理，带来推理成本和延迟，但直接加长度惩罚又容易损伤复杂题表现。","importance":"这篇工作对预训练研究的直接关系不强，但对 RL 后训练和推理成本控制有参考价值。它的关键点是把长度控制从 reward shaping 转为 rollout 后的样本重采样。这样做避免了手调长度惩罚系数，也尽量保留 correctness reward 的主导地位。对研究 RL 训练动态的人，QLPO 的价值在于它把 group-based RL 中的梯度来源拆成两个层面：group 内 reward 统计决定优势分配，具体 trajectory 决定 token 级梯度如何落到模型上。","technical_approach":"QLPO 是 GRPO 的重采样变体。对每个 prompt，先从旧策略过采样 K 条候选 response，而不是直接用 M 条 response 更新。然后按正确性分成正样本和负样本，并保留过采样池中的经验正确/错误比例：N+(x)=round(M·N~+(x)/K)，N−(x)=M−N+(x)。接着在每个正确性类别内部按长度划分 short 和 long，再用 quadrant-weighted sampling 选择最终训练组。策略是偏向采样 short correct 和 long incorrect。原因是，在 GRPO 中正确样本通常获得正 advantage，会被强化；因此强化短且正确的轨迹会鼓励简洁解法。错误样本通常获得负 advantage，会被压低；选择长且错误的轨迹，相当于把“冗长但失败”的模式作为负梯度来源。它不修改 reward、advantage estimator 或后续 GRPO 更新流程。相比只选最短 response 的过滤方法，QLPO 保留了负样本的梯度作用，也减少了因为改变正负比例而扭曲 group-relative advantage 的风险。","experiments":"作者报告在 1.5B 到 32B 参数规模、base model 和已有 reasoning model 上做了实验，任务覆盖数学和科学类 benchmark。主要结论是 QLPO 能改善 accuracy–length frontier：在保持竞争性准确率的同时减少 response length。文中还强调它比显式长度惩罚更稳健，因为不需要调惩罚系数；也比简单 shortest-only 过滤更可靠，因为后者可能忽略错误样本的负梯度，甚至降低推理效率。给出的内容未包含完整表格数值，因此不宜补写具体百分比。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 15–20 group–trajectory gradient analysis 是最值得看的部分，它解释了为什么采样 long incorrect 不是反直觉操作。实验部分重点扫 main accuracy-length frontier、与 length penalty/GFPO 的对比、不同模型规模的稳定性，以及 over-generation K 和 quadrant 权重的消融。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses length explosion in RL-trained reasoning models. The comparison spans RNNs, softmax transformers, linear-attention transformers, and SSMs, yielding architecture-dependent expressivity boundaries conditioned on token order.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这类 paper 不会直接改 recipe，但会改你对 SSM/linear attention 失分来源的 prior；重点看 indexing-at-end 的分离结论。","en":"It won’t change your recipe tomorrow, but it should update your prior on where SSM/linear-attention losses come from; focus on the indexing-at-end separation."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Alexander Kozachinskiy","Vicente Opazo","Felipe Urrutia"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["indexing","theory","SSM","linear-attention","transformer"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22361","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.22361","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.21613","title":"The Hard Decision Layer: Evidence for Committed Inference in Transformers","abstract":"We investigate where and how transformer-based language models commit to predictions in multiple-choice question answering. We identify the _Hard Decision Layer_ (HDL), a natural architectural property where answer option rankings stabilize abruptly during inference. fine-tuni","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-25T03:54:43Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2607_21613.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的问题很具体：Transformer 在做多项选择推断时，究竟在哪一层“把答案定下来”。已有 early exit 或 layer pruning 工作更多是在做外部控制，或者事后裁剪；这篇文章关心的是模型内部是否天然存在一个稳定的决策边界。作者在 MCQA 场景下提出 Hard Decision Layer（HDL）：从这一层开始，候选答案的相对排序会突然稳定，后续层主要是在放大已有偏好，而不是继续改写答案次序。论文试图说明，这不是 learned router、不是微调副产物，而是标准 Transformer 推理过程里可重复观察到的结构性现象。","importance":"对基座研究的直接价值不在于提出了新的训练方法，而在于给“深度上的计算分工”提供了一个可操作的观测对象。若 HDL 现象稳健成立，它会影响三类问题。第一，训练动态与机制分析：它提示模型的决策并不是均匀分布在所有层，而是存在一个中后层的相变点。第二，推理效率：如果排序在 HDL 后已基本冻结，那么面向分类式或 constrained decoding 任务，静态 early exit 可能比学习式路由更简单。第三，后训练与对齐分析：作者发现 HDL 对 LoRA 微调基本不敏感，这意味着不少任务适配可能主要改写局部表征或最终 margin，而不改变“何时做决定”的层级结构。贡献量级偏机制观察与诊断工具，不是范式级方法创新，但对理解 layerwise reasoning 有一定参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法上并不复杂，核心是把 Logit Lens 用在多项选择答案 token 的层间排序轨迹上。具体做法是：对每个样本，在一次前向中缓存所有层的 residual hidden states；对每层最后位置的 hidden state 先过 final layer norm，再用 unembedding 投到词表空间；只取答案标签 token（如 A/B/C/D）的 logits，得到每层的候选排序。随后作者不是只看 top-1 是否变化，而是看排序何时整体稳定，并据此定义 HDL。这个定义比单纯的 layerwise accuracy 更强，因为它要求相对偏好结构固定，而不是偶然 top-1 对了。\n\n论文还做了几件补充分析来支撑“HDL 是结构属性”这个说法。其一，跨模型与跨数据集验证：Qwen、Llama、Granite、Mistral 上都能看到类似的中层到后层突变。其二，LoRA 微调前后 HDL 位置近似不变，说明它不太像任务头或微调模板诱导出来的现象。其三，改变选项数和标签形式，发现 alphabetic 与阿拉伯数字标签更容易形成清晰边界，roman numeral 较差，这说明 HDL 的可观测性部分依赖 tokenizer 与标签 token 的表征几何。其四，作者还分析了最终输出 rank 分组后的平均层间轨迹，试图说明最终胜出的 token 往往在 HDL 附近完成与其他选项的分离。\n\n和已有工作相比，这篇文章的区别在于它不训练退出策略，也不主张删层，而是把“决策承诺”当成一个自然发生的层级现象来测量。缺点也在这里：它依赖 Logit Lens，而 Logit Lens 对中间层语义的可解释性一直有争议；另外它把答案限制为单 token 标签，这让分析干净，但也离开放式生成较远。","experiments":"实验覆盖 4 个模型和 4 个 MCQA 数据集，重点结论是：HDL 在不同架构上都能观察到，而且 layerwise accuracy 往往在 HDL 附近出现明显跃升，之后基本平台化。摘要里给出的最大例子是 Qwen 在 CommonsenseQA 上，HDL 处相对早层可达到 +0.61 的准确率提升，随后层间变化很小。这个结果支持“后续层更多是在强化而不是改判”。\n\n消融方面，最有信息量的是三组。第一，标签形式消融：A/B/C/D 和 1/2/3/4 的 HDL 更清晰，罗马数字标签更弱，说明 token identity 与预训练频率会影响层间可读出的决策边界。第二，选项数变化后 HDL 仍存在，说明它不是四选一模板的偶然产物。第三，LoRA 微调前后 HDL 基本不变，支持其架构内生性。论文还提到问题复杂度变化不会消除 HDL，只会影响边界锐度和准确率水平。\n\n从基座研究角度，最该留意的不是单个数字，而是两个模式：一是排序稳定通常是突变而不是平滑收敛；二是最终正确答案的 margin 扩张与排序冻结并不同步，后层仍可能继续拉大 logit gap。这意味着“决定何时做出”和“置信度何时形成”可能是两个不同过程。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不必逐式推导。先看 Introduction，明确作者要区分的是“自然决策层”与“学习式 early exit”。然后重点看 Methodology 里 HDL 的 operational definition：他们到底把“稳定”定义成 top-1 不变，还是完整排序不变，以及统计是按样本、按数据集还是按最终 rank 分组做的。接着看主实验图，尤其是 layerwise accuracy / rank stabilization 曲线，确认 HDL 是尖锐转折还是宽平台。再看标签格式和 LoRA 消融，这两部分决定了结论能否从“prompt artifact”提升到“结构现象”。\n\n如果只花 20–30 分钟，建议看：引言、HDL useful question: at which layer does a transformer actually commit to an answer in multiple-choice reasoning? Prior work on early exit or layer pruning mostly adds external control or performs post-hoc compression. Here the focus is different: whether standard transformers exhibit an intrinsic decision boundary during inference. The proposed Hard Decision Layer (HDL) is the layer after which the ranking of answer options becomes stable, suggesting that later layers mostly amplify an already chosen preference rather than continue changing the decision.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is indirect but real. The paper does not introduce a new training recipe; instead, it offers a concrete probe for depth-wise computation allocation. If HDL is robust, it matters for three reasons. First, it sharpens our picture of training dynamics and internal computation: decision formation may be concentrated around a mid-to-late layer transition rather than spread uniformly across depth. Second, it has implications for inference efficiency on classification-style tasks, where static early exit might be feasible without learned routers. Third, the reported invariance under LoRA fine-tuning suggests that task adaptation may often modify margins or local representations without changing the depth at which commitment happens. However, we find that frontier reasoning models sometimes possess the necessary capabilities to solve problems but fail due to premature self-doubt -- a phenomenon informally known as context anxiety. We provide the first systematic study of context anxiety, demonstrating that it arises, in part, from a model's inability to accurately estimate the tokens required to complete a task. We also show that","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-29T18:41:22Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2607_21616.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究的不是模型“不会做题”，而是模型在长程推理任务里错误地判断自己“做不完”，于是提前放弃、绕路、或者生成更低效的解法。作者把这种现象定义为 context anxiety。更具体地说，核心问题是：当前 reasoning model 对完成任务所需 token 的自我估计存在系统性失准，这种失准会诱发行为层面的退缩，而不是能力层面的失败。","importance":"对预训练和后训练接口都有启发。它提醒人们，长上下文与长链推理的瓶颈不一定只来自容量、架构或算力，也可能来自模型对自身生成成本的错误表征。这个问题如果成立，就意味着一部分“推理能力不足”其实是 policy 问题，而不是 capability 问题。对 post-train 更直接：SFT 或 RL 不只是教模型更会推理，也可能在重塑它对资源约束的内隐估计。对 pretrain 的间接启发是，训练目标里几乎没有显式校准“完成任务需要多长输出”的信号，模型学到的是语言续写分布，不是 generation budget awareness。","technical_approach":"方法分三步。第一步是检测 context anxiety。作者不是只靠关键词匹配，而是用一个 LLM judge panel 去判定 reasoning trace 中是否存在“任务超出上下文/ token 限制”的语义表达，并据此把样本分成 anxiety-driven failures、capability-driven failures 和 successful attempts。这个划分很关键，因为它把“没做对”拆成了两种不同机制。第二步是测量 token 使用校准。作者让模型在完成回答后，自己估计刚才完成该解答需要多少 token，再用真实 token 数做比值，构造 winsorized token ratio。这个量不是在测 tokenizer 知识，而是在测模型主观感受到的生成成本。若该比值系统性偏大，说明模型倾向于高估完成任务所需篇幅。第三步是干预。作者构造一个蒸馏式 SFT 数据集：从目标模型和更强教师模型中收集成功且不表现 context anxiety 的 reasoning traces，只保留完成任务且无焦虑表达的样本，再对学生模型做轻量监督微调。这里的设计点在于，训练目标不是直接灌答案，而是让模型模仿“能完成长任务且不自我退缩”的推理策略。与常见提升推理的做法相比，这篇工作关注的是行为校准，而不是增加搜索、投票或更长 test-time compute。","experiments":"主实验围绕 Tower of Hanoi 展开，并在 shortest-path grid search 上做了验证。论文报告，出现 context anxiety 的前沿推理模型，其 token 需求估计平均失准约 24%；这种失准与 15% 的准确率下降相关，并且在成功解题时还会带来约 54% 的 token 使用增加，说明焦虑不仅导致放弃，也会让成功样本变得更啰嗦、更低效。作者还指出，这种现象出现在具备长上下文和动态长度适配能力的模型上，不是简单的 context window 太短。干预方面，轻量 SFT 能把 context anxiety 降低 50% 以上，支持“这是可塑的行为策略，而非固定能力上限”的结论。泛读时要注意两个实验细节：一是 anxiety detector 的人工验证和敏感性分析是否足够可靠；二是 token ratio 与准确率、放弃行为之间是相关还是有更强的中介证据。若全文给了回归或 mediation analysis，这部分比 headline 数字更值得看。","reading_guide":"先看 Introduction，把作者要区分的两类失败记住：能力不够，和误以为自己做不完。然后直接看方法部分关于 anxiety detection 和 winsorized token ratio 的定义，这是全文最有新意的地方。实验部分优先看 Tower of Hanoi 的主结果表，再看 shortest-path 的外部验证，以及 SFT 干预前后的变化。预计 25 分钟左右能抓住主线。若想判断这项工作是否扎实，重点检查 Appendix 里的 judge 可靠性、人类标注一致性、以及 token lacking reasoning ability. In long-horizon tasks, models sometimes incorrectly conclude that they cannot finish within their token budget, then give up early, hedge, or switch to inefficient solution strategies. The authors call this context anxiety. More precisely, the paper argues that some reasoning failures are driven by systematic misestimation of the tokens required to complete a task, which then triggers behavioral disengagement rather than genuine incapacity.","importance":"This matters for both pretraining and post-training. It suggests that some apparent limits in long-context reasoning are not purely about model size, architecture, or compute. They may come from a learned policy that misjudges generation cost. If that is true, then part of what looks like a capability ceiling is actually a calibration problem. The post-training implication is immediate: SFT or RL may improve reasoning not only by teaching better search patterns, but also by reshaping the model’s internal estimate of how much effort a task requires. The pretraining implication is indirect but important: standard LM objectives do not explicitly teach budget awareness or accurate self-estimation of completion length.","technical_approach":"$1c","experiments":"$1d","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction and keep the paper’s main distinction in mind: inability versus mistaken self-limitation. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-07T22:31:23Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2607_21627.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文关注复合 prompt 相对 neutral prompt 对 next-token distribution 的影响。具体地，对同一个生成历史 h 和 token v，计算 u_r,θ(h,v)=log pθ(v|h,s_r)-log pθ(v|h,s_0)。这个 log-ratio 表示 role prompt 如何重新加权模型的默认预测：正值说明角色提示提高该 token 概率，负值说明压低该 token 概率。Role Drift 被定义为 RL 过程中这个 role utility 偏离 pre-RL 值。Role Anchor 在 policy gradient 目标中加入正则，约束当前 role utility 不要远离训练前的 role utility。它不改系统架构，也不需要为每个模块人工标注细粒度行为标签。和普通 KL 不同，它锚定的是“role prompt 相对 neutral prompt 的差异”，因此保留的是角色提示带来的行为方向，而不是简单限制整个策略不更新。作者还用梯度分析说明，Role Anchor 主要是把更新从角色违规方向上拉开，而不是整体压制学习。","experiments":"实验覆盖两个复合系统。第一个是多跳推理中的 Decomposer-Solver pipeline。Decomposer gain disappearance 的分析。梯度分析值得扫一遍，它解释了 Role Anchor 为什么不是简单减小更新步长。Appendix A.1 的 utility 推导适合想确认 log-ratio 合理性的读者。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies role drift in compound LLM systems: after end-to-end RL, the final answer accuracy can improve while individual modules stop following their intended roles. A decomposer may leak answer entities, and a reader may ignore retrieved evidence and rely on parametric memory.","importance":"The main contribution is a clean diagnosis of a training-signal mismatch in outcome-only RL. A terminal reward constrains the final answer, but it does not constrain where the computation happens inside a modular system. This matters for systems whose value comes from grounding, auditability, parallelism, or module replaceability. The paper gives a measurable notion of drift and a lightweight regularizer, making it useful as a practical tool for RL training of compound LLM systems.","technical_approach":"The proposed method, Role Anchor, defines a module’s role through the effect of its role prompt relative to a neutral prompt. For the same history h and token v, the role utility is u_r,θ(h,v)=log pθ(v|h,s_r)-log pθ(v|h,s_0). This log-ratio measures how the role prompt reweights the module’s default next-token distribution. Role drift is then a change in this utility during end-to-end optimization. Role Anchor adds a regularization term to the policy-gradient objective to keep the current role utility close to its pre-RL value. This differs from a standard KL constraint: it anchors the effect of the role prompt, not the whole policy distribution. The gradient analysis argues that the method redirects updates away from role-violating directions rather than simply suppressing learning.","experiments":"The paper evaluates two compound pipelines. In a multi-hop reasoning system with a Decomposer and a Solver, outcome-only RL improves final accuracy, but the Decomposer increasingly plants answer entities in its sub-questions. When the Decomposer is constrained to stay within its role, 86% of the apparent gain disappears, indicating that much of the improvement came from role violation. In a retrieval setting with a query generator, a frozen retriever, and a Reader, RL again improves accuracy, but the Reader becomes less responsive to retrieved passages and relies more on parametric knowledge. Role Anchor makes this hidden loss of grounding observable and controllable.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 15–25 minutes. Start with the two examples in the introduction, since they make clear why final accuracy is insufficient. Then read the formal definition of role utility and Role Anchor; the role-prompt versus neutral-prompt log-ratio is the key technical object. In the experiments, focus on how answer leakage and passage sensitivity are measured, and on the 86% disappearance of the apparent gain. The gradient analysis is worth reading to understand why the method is not just a smaller RL update. Appendix A.1 is useful if you want the inference-as-optimization justification for the log-ratio utility.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看 failure cases；它把“reward 变好但机制变坏”做成可测信号，§实验里的角色捷径比正则本身更有用。","en":"Worth reading for the failure cases: it turns “better reward, worse mechanism” into a measurable signal, and the role-shortcut examples matter more than the regularizer."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Xiaoyang Cao","Siddarth Srinivasan","Michiel A.
